   }

                                       ...  
   }

   ...
   }

   ...
   }

}

   vehicle        LaneAttributes-Vehicle,           crosswalk      LaneAttributes-Crosswalk,         bikeLane       LaneAttributes-Bike,              sidewalk       LaneAttributes-Sidewalk,          median         LaneAttributes-Barrier,           striping       LaneAttributes-Striping,          trackedVehicle LaneAttributes-TrackedVehicle,    parking        LaneAttributes-Parking,           ...
   } 


OverlayLaneList ::= SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..5)) OF Common.LaneID
               

PreemptPriorityList ::= SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..32)) OF SignalControlZone

SignalControlZone ::=  SEQUENCE {
  zone  RegionalExtension {{REGION.Reg-SignalControlZone}},
  ...
  }


RestrictionClassAssignment ::= SEQUENCE {
   id       Common.RestrictionClassID,
                           users    RestrictionUserTypeList 
                           }


RestrictionClassList ::= SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..254)) OF RestrictionClassAssignment


RestrictionUserTypeList ::= SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..16)) OF  RestrictionUserType


RestrictionUserType ::= CHOICE {
   basicType   RestrictionAppliesTo,
                  regional    SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..4)) OF 
               RegionalExtension {{REGION.Reg-RestrictionUserType}}, 
   ...
   }


RoadLaneSetList ::= SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..255)) OF GenericLane


RoadSegmentList ::= SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..32)) OF RoadSegment

   
RoadSegment ::= SEQUENCE {
   name        Common.DescriptiveName OPTIONAL,
   id          Common.RoadSegmentReferenceID,
                              revision    Common.MsgCount,    
      refPoint    Common.Position3D,                                                          laneWidth   Common.LaneWidth OPTIONAL,   
                                                           speedLimits Common.SpeedLimitList OPTIONAL, 
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
               roadLaneSet RoadLaneSetList,   
  
   regional    SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..4)) OF 
               RegionalExtension {{REGION.Reg-RoadSegment}} OPTIONAL,
   ...
   }

AllowedManeuvers ::= BIT STRING {
                    maneuverStraightAllowed      (0), 
                                      maneuverLeftAllowed          (1),  
                                     maneuverRightAllowed         (2), 
                                     maneuverUTurnAllowed         (3), 
                                     maneuverLeftTurnOnRedAllowed (4), 
                                                                      maneuverRightTurnOnRedAllowed (5), 
                                                                      maneuverLaneChangeAllowed    (6), 
                                                                                                       maneuverNoStoppingAllowed    (7), 
                                                                      yieldAllwaysRequired         (8), 
                                                                      goWithHalt                   (9), 
                                     caution                      (10), 
                                     reserved1                    (11)
                                    
   } (SIZE(12))  
 
LaneAttributes-Barrier ::= BIT STRING {
      median-RevocableLane     (0),
                                                                                       median                   (1),
   whiteLineHashing         (2),
   stripedLines             (3),
   doubleStripedLines       (4),
   trafficCones             (5),
   constructionBarrier      (6),
   trafficChannels          (7),
   lowCurbs                 (8),
   highCurbs                (9)
      } (SIZE (16))

LaneAttributes-Bike ::= BIT STRING {
      bikeRevocableLane       (0),
                                                                                    pedestrianUseAllowed    (1),
                                                         isBikeFlyOverLane       (2),
                              fixedCycleTime          (3),
                                                         biDirectionalCycleTimes (4),
                                                                                                                                                                                                                           isolatedByBarrier       (5),
   unsignalizedSegmentsPresent  (6)
                                                      
      } (SIZE (16))

LaneAttributes-Crosswalk ::= BIT STRING { 
         crosswalkRevocableLane  (0),
                                                                                    bicyleUseAllowed        (1),
                                                         isXwalkFlyOverLane      (2),
                              fixedCycleTime          (3),
                                                         biDirectionalCycleTimes (4),
                                                                                                                                                                                                                           hasPushToWalkButton     (5),
                              audioSupport            (6),
                              rfSignalRequestPresent  (7),
                              unsignalizedSegmentsPresent  (8)
                                                            } (SIZE (16))

LaneAttributes-Parking ::= BIT STRING { 
                parkingRevocableLane         (0),
                                                                                                       parallelParkingInUse         (1),
    headInParkingInUse           (2),
    doNotParkZone                (3),
                                                                      parkingForBusUse             (4),
    parkingForTaxiUse            (5),
    noPublicParkingUse           (6)
                                                                        } (SIZE (16))

LaneAttributes-Sidewalk ::= BIT STRING { 
      sidewalk-RevocableLane  (0),
                                                                                    bicyleUseAllowed        (1),
                                                         isSidewalkFlyOverLane   (2),
                              walkBikes               (3)
                                 } (SIZE (16))


LaneAttributes-Striping ::= BIT STRING { 
       stripeToConnectingLanesRevocableLane      (0),
                         stripeDrawOnLeft                          (1),
    stripeDrawOnRight                         (2),
            stripeToConnectingLanesLeft               (3),
    stripeToConnectingLanesRight              (4),
    stripeToConnectingLanesAhead              (5)
                                              } (SIZE (16))


LaneAttributes-TrackedVehicle ::= BIT STRING { 
      spec-RevocableLane         (0),
                                                                                                spec-commuterRailRoadTrack (1), 
   spec-lightRailRoadTrack    (2),
   spec-heavyRailRoadTrack    (3),
   spec-otherRailType         (4)
      } (SIZE (16))


LaneAttributes-Vehicle ::= BIT STRING {  
      isVehicleRevocableLane       (0),
                                                                                                      isVehicleFlyOverLane         (1),
                                    hovLaneUseOnly               (2),
   restrictedToBusUse           (3),
   restrictedToTaxiUse          (4),
   restrictedFromPublicUse      (5),
   hasIRbeaconCoverage          (6),
   permissionOnRequest          (7) 
   } (SIZE (8,...))

LaneDirection ::= BIT STRING {
            ingressPath     (0), 
                                         egressPath      (1)
                                                        } (SIZE (2))

LaneSharing ::= BIT STRING {
        overlappingLaneDescriptionProvided  (0),
                multipleLanesTreatedAsOneLane       (1),
        
        otherNonMotorizedTrafficTypes       (2),     individualMotorizedVehicleTraffic   (3),
    busVehicleTraffic                   (4),  
    taxiVehicleTraffic                  (5),
    pedestriansTraffic                  (6),
    cyclistVehicleTraffic               (7),
    trackedVehicleTraffic               (8),
    reserved                            (9)
    } (SIZE (10))
    
LayerID ::= INTEGER (0..100)

LayerType ::= ENUMERATED {
     none, 
     mixedContent,      generalMapData, 
     intersectionData, 
     curveData, 
     roadwaySectionData, 
     parkingAreaData, 
     sharedLaneData,
     ... 
     } 

RestrictionAppliesTo ::= ENUMERATED {
    none,                  equippedTransit,       equippedTaxis,
    equippedOther,                                                       emissionCompliant,                            equippedBicycle,
    weightCompliant,
    heightCompliant,
            pedestrians,
    slowMovingPersons,
    wheelchairUsers,
    visualDisabilities,
    audioDisabilities,      otherUnknownDisabilities,
    ...
    }
END

   &Type
   } WITH SYNTAX {&Type IDENTIFIED BY &id}

   request        SignalRequest,
                                                      
      minute         Common.MinuteOfTheYear OPTIONAL,
   second         Common.DSecond OPTIONAL,
   duration       Common.DSecond OPTIONAL,
                                                                                                                                                                                                         
   regional       SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..4)) OF 
                  RegionalExtension {{REGION.Reg-SignalRequestPackage}} OPTIONAL,
   ...  
   }

SignalRequest ::= SEQUENCE {
      id            Common.IntersectionReferenceID,  
   
      requestID     Common.RequestID, 
  
         requestType   PriorityRequestType,   
   
            inBoundLane   Common.IntersectionAccessPoint,       
                    outBoundLane  Common.IntersectionAccessPoint OPTIONAL,       
                                                      regional      SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..4)) OF 
                 RegionalExtension {{REGION.Reg-SignalRequest}} OPTIONAL,
   ...
   }

DeltaTime ::= INTEGER (-122 .. 121) 
            
PriorityRequestType ::= ENUMERATED {
     priorityRequestTypeReserved (0),  
     priorityRequest             (1),   
     priorityRequestUpdate       (2), 
     priorityCancellation        (3),  
     ...  
     }

TransitVehicleOccupancy ::= ENUMERATED {
    occupancyUnknown    (0),  
    occupancyEmpty      (1),  
    occupancyVeryLow    (2),  
    occupancyLow        (3),  
    occupancyMed        (4),  
    occupancyHigh       (5),  
    occupancyNearlyFull (6),  
    occupancyFull       (7)
    }

TransitVehicleStatus ::= BIT STRING {
    loading     (0),     anADAuse    (1),     aBikeLoad   (2),     doorOpen    (3),     charging    (4),     atStopLine  (5)      } (SIZE(8))
END

TrafficLightStatusMessage
{joint-iso-itu-t (2) country (16) us (840) organization (1) sae (114566) 
v2x-communications (1) technical-committees (1) v2x-core (5) technical-reports (1) 
j2735 (2735) part-0 (0) asn1-module (1) trafficLightStatusMessage (50) 
version-1 (1) version-minor-1 (1)}

DEFINITIONS AUTOMATIC TAGS ::=
BEGIN

IMPORTS
    DDateTime,
    Latitude,
    Longitude
        FROM Common {joint-iso-itu-t (2) country (16) us (840) organization (1) sae (114566)
		v2x-communications (1) technical-committees (1) v2x-core (5) technical-reports (1) 
		j2735 (2735) part-0 (0) asn1-module (1) common (38) 
		version-1 (1)  version-minor-1 (1)} WITH SUCCESSORS;

TrafficLightStatusMessage ::= SEQUENCE { 
    trafficLightID       TrafficLightID, 
            long                Common.Longitude   OPTIONAL,
            lat                 Common.Latitude    OPTIONAL,
            operationStatus     TrafficLightOperationStatus,
        controllerStatus    TrafficLightControllerStatus,
        cyclecounter        TimeInSecond-B16, 
        cycletime           TimeInSecond-B16, 
        currentTime         Common.DDateTime,
        trafficLightStatus  TrafficLightStatusList,
        ...
}

TrafficLightStatusList ::= SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..255)) OF TrafficLightStatus

TrafficLightStatus ::= SEQUENCE {
    trafficLightType            TrafficLightType,
            consecutiveTrafficLight     ConsecutiveTrafficLight,
                    trafficLightIntervalType    TrafficLightIntervalType,
        pedestrianCall              PedestrianCall,
        actuatedinterval            ActuatedInterval,
        permissiveNonProtected      PermissiveNonProtected,
        lightingStatus              TrafficLightingStatus,
        maxIntervalLength           TimeInSecond-B8,
        remainingTime               TimeInSecond-B8,
        directionCode               TrafficLightDirectionCode,
        reserved                    ReservedBit,
        ...
}

ActuatedInterval ::= BOOLEAN

ConsecutiveTrafficLight ::= ENUMERATED {
    none    (0),
    first   (1),
    second  (2),
    ...
}

PedestrianCall ::= BOOLEAN 

PermissiveNonProtected ::= ENUMERATED {
    none        (0),      permissive  (1),      ...
}

ReservedBit ::= BOOLEAN

TimeInSecond-B16 ::= INTEGER (0..65535)

TimeInSecond-B8 ::= INTEGER (0..255)

TrafficLightControllerStatus ::= BIT STRING {
    conflict            (0), 
                    center-comm-error   (1), 
                    scu-comm-error      (2), 
                            reserved1           (3), 
        reserved2           (4), 
    reserved3           (5),
    reserved4           (6),
    reserved5           (7)
} (SIZE(8, ...))

TrafficLightDirectionCode ::= INTEGER (0..359) 
TrafficLightID ::= CHOICE {
    id  INTEGER,                oid OBJECT IDENTIFIER   }

TrafficLightIntervalType ::= ENUMERATED {
    static      (0),      variable    (1),      ...
}

TrafficLightOperationStatus ::= BIT STRING {
    manual          (0),
        flashing        (1),
        off             (2),
                actuated        (3),
            transition      (4),
            priority        (5),
            holding-phase   (6),
            reserved        (7)
    } (SIZE(8, ...))

TrafficLightingStatus ::= ENUMERATED {
    off             (0),
    red-on          (1),
    yellow-on       (2),
    green-on        (3),
    red-blinking    (4),
    yellow-blinking (5),
    green-blinking  (6),
    ...
}

TrafficLightType ::= ENUMERATED {
    reserved        (0),
    straight        (1),
    left-turn       (2),
    pedestrian      (3),
    bicycle         (4),
    right-turn      (5),
    bus             (6),
    u-turn          (7),
    ...
}

END

   &id     PartII-Id UNIQUE,
   &Type
   } WITH SYNTAX {&Type IDENTIFIED BY &id}

CCMPartIIcontent  { PARTII-EXT-ID-AND-TYPE: Set} ::= SEQUENCE {
   partII-Id     PARTII-EXT-ID-AND-TYPE.&id( {Set} ),
   partII-Value  PARTII-EXT-ID-AND-TYPE.&Type( {Set}{@partII-Id} )
   }

   vehicleSafetyExt       PartII-Id::= 0     lightVehicleCCMExt PartII-Id::= 1     heavyTruckCCMExt PartII-Id::= 2

   { BasicSafetyMessage.VehicleSafetyExtensions      IDENTIFIED BY vehicleSafetyExt} |
   { LightVehicleCCMExtensions    IDENTIFIED BY lightVehicleCCMExt} |

technical-reports (1) j3217 (3217) asn1-module (1) tollUsageMessage (1) 
version-1 (1) version-minor-1 (1)}

DEFINITIONS AUTOMATIC TAGS ::=
BEGIN

IMPORTS 
    VehicleTypes, 
    TollChargerInfo,
    TollPointID
        FROM TollAdvertisementMessage {joint-iso-itu-t (2) country (16) us (840)
        organization (1) sae (114566) v2x-communications (1) technical-committees (1)
        tolling-applications (9) technical-reports (1) j3217 (3217) asn1-module (1)
        tollAdvertisementMessage (0) version-1 (1) version-minor-1 (1)} WITH SUCCESSORS
    GenericLane
        FROM MapData {joint-iso-itu-t (2) country (16) us (840) organization (1)
        sae (114566) v2x-communications (1) technical-committees (1) v2x-core (5)
        technical-reports (1) j2735 (2735) part-0 (0) asn1-module (1) mapData (8)
        version-1 (1) version-minor-1 (1)} WITH SUCCESSORS
    DescriptiveName,
    DDateTime,
    LaneID,
    Latitude,
    Longitude,
    Elevation,
    MinuteOfTheYear,
    MsgCount,
    Position3D,
    TemporaryID
        FROM Common {joint-iso-itu-t (2) country (16) us (840) organization (1)
        sae (114566) v2x-communications (1) technical-committees (1) v2x-core (5)
        technical-reports (1) j2735 (2735) part-0 (0) asn1-module (1) common (38)
        version-1 (1) version-minor-1 (1)} WITH SUCCESSORS
    EngineCharacteristics,
    PaymentFee,
    UserId,
    VehicleDescription
        FROM EfcDataDictionary {iso(1) standard(0) 17573 dd(3) 
        version1(1)} WITH SUCCESSORS;


TollUsageMessage ::= SEQUENCE {                   
    tollPointInfo       TollAdvertisementMessage.TollChargerInfo,        tempID              Common.TemporaryID,                                  
    tumSequenceNum      Common.MsgCount,               tamSequenceNum      Common.MsgCount,               tumHash             TumHash OPTIONAL,
    encryptedTumData    EncryptedTumData,       ...
}

TumHash ::= OCTET STRING (SIZE(32)) EncryptedTumData ::= OCTET STRING (SIZE(1..7000)) 

TumData ::= SEQUENCE {
    tollUserData                TollUserData,
    tollServiceProviderData     TollServiceProviderData OPTIONAL,
    ...
}

TollServiceProviderData ::= OCTET STRING (SIZE(1..1024, ...))

TollUserData ::= SEQUENCE {
    timeStamp           Common.DDateTime,                                                                   
    tspId               OBJECT IDENTIFIER,      vehicleId           VehicleId, 
    vehType             TollAdvertisementMessage.VehicleTypes        OPTIONAL,
    vehicleDescription  EfcDataDictionary.VehicleDescription  OPTIONAL,           vehAxlesAndWeight   VehicleAxlesAndWeightInfo OPTIONAL,
    numOccupants        INTEGER (2..5, ...)      OPTIONAL, 
        entryTollPointId    TollAdvertisementMessage.TollPointID         OPTIONAL,
    entryTimeStamp      Common.DDateTime           OPTIONAL,
        locAndTimeStamps    LocAndTimeStamps    OPTIONAL,
    lastTollPoints      LastTollPointInfos  OPTIONAL, 
        charge              EfcDataDictionary.PaymentFee          OPTIONAL,                                         
    ...
}

VehicleAxlesAndWeightInfo ::= SEQUENCE {
    vehNumAxles         INTEGER (1..10, ...), 
        vehWeightPerAxle    SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..10, ...)) OF INTEGER (0..65535),
        vehTotalWeight      INTEGER (0..16777215), 
        vehWeightUnits      ENUMERATED {pounds, kilograms, ...},
    ...
}

VehicleId ::= SEQUENCE {
    vehicleIdentity     OCTET STRING (SIZE(16, ...)) OPTIONAL,
    licPlateState       IA5String (SIZE(2))          OPTIONAL,                             
    licPlateNumVeh      IA5String (SIZE(1..12, ...)) OPTIONAL,
    licPlateNumTrailer  IA5String (SIZE(1..12, ...)) OPTIONAL, 
        userId              EfcDataDictionary.UserId                       OPTIONAL,     ...
}

LocAndTimeStamps ::= SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..5, ...)) OF LocAndTimeStamp

LocAndTimeStamp ::= SEQUENCE {
    latitude    Common.Latitude,
    longitude   Common.Longitude,
    elevation   Common.Elevation   OPTIONAL,
    timeStamp   Common.DDateTime,
    currentLane Common.LaneID      OPTIONAL,
    ...
}

LastTollPointInfos ::= SEQUENCE (SIZE (1..5, ...)) OF LastTollPointInfo

LastTollPointInfo ::= SEQUENCE {
    tollChargerInfo     TollAdvertisementMessage.TollChargerInfo,    
        timeStamp           Common.DDateTime,          
        ...
}

END







SensorDataSharingMessage 
{joint-iso-itu-t (2) country (16) us (840) organization (1) sae (114566) 
v2x-communications (1) technical-committees (1) v2x-vehicular-applications (8) 
technical-reports (1) j3224 (3224) asn1-module (1) sensorDataSharingMessage (41) 
version-1 (1) version-minor-1 (1)}

DEFINITIONS AUTOMATIC TAGS ::=
BEGIN

IMPORTS
    Acceleration,
    AccelerationSet4Way,
    BasicVehicleClass,
    DDateTime,
    Elevation,
    ElevationConfidence,
    ExteriorLights,
    Heading,
    HeadingConfidence,
    Latitude,
    Longitude,
    MsgCount,
    Position3D,
    PositionalAccuracy,
    PositionConfidenceSet,
    SemiMajorAxisAccuracy,
    SemiMinorAxisAccuracy,
    SemiMajorAxisOrientation,
    Speed,
    SpeedConfidence,
    TemporaryID,
    TimeConfidence,
    VehicleHeight,
    VehicleLength,
    VehicleSize,
    VehicleWidth,
    VerticalAcceleration,
    YawRate
        FROM Common {joint-iso-itu-t (2) country (16) us (840) organization (1) sae (114566) 
		v2x-communications (1) technical-committees (1) v2x-core (5) technical-reports (1) 
		j2735 (2735) part-0 (0) asn1-module (1) common (38) 
		version-1 (1)  version-minor-1 (1)} WITH SUCCESSORS
    Attachment,
    AttachmentRadius,
    PersonalDeviceUserType,
    PropelledInformation
        FROM PersonalSafetyMessage {joint-iso-itu-t (2) country (16) us (840) organization (1) sae (114566)
        v2x-communications (1) technical-committees (1) v2x-core (5) technical-reports (1)
        j2735 (2735) part-0 (0) asn1-module (1) personalSafetyMessage (21) 
        version-1 (1) version-minor-1 (1)} WITH SUCCESSORS
    AccelerationConfidence,
    YawRateConfidence
        FROM ProbeVehicleData {joint-iso-itu-t (2) country (16) us (840) organization (1) sae (114566)
        v2x-communications (1) technical-committees (1) v2x-core (5) technical-reports (1)
        j2735 (2735) part-0 (0) asn1-module (1) probeVehicleData (15) 
        version-1 (1) version-minor-1 (1)} WITH SUCCESSORS;

SensorDataSharingMessage ::= SEQUENCE {
    msgCnt Common.MsgCount,
        sourceID        Common.TemporaryID,
        equipmentType   EquipmentType,
        sDSMTimeStamp   Common.DDateTime,
        refPos          Common.Position3D,
        refPosXYConf    Common.PositionalAccuracy,
    refPosElConf    Common.ElevationConfidence     OPTIONAL,
    objects DetectedObjectList
    }

EquipmentType ::= ENUMERATED{
    unknown (0),
    rsu     (1),
    obu     (2),
    vru     (3),
    ...
}

DetectedObjectList::= SEQUENCE (SIZE(1..256)) OF DetectedObjectData

DetectedObjectData::= SEQUENCE {
    detObjCommon    DetectedObjectCommonData,
        detObjOptData   DetectedObjectOptionalData OPTIONAL
    }

DetectedObjectOptionalData ::= CHOICE {
    detVeh      DetectedVehicleData,
        detVRU      DetectedVRUData,
        detObst     DetectedObstacleData
    }

DetectedObjectCommonData::= SEQUENCE {
    objType             ObjectType,
    objTypeCfd          ClassificationConfidence,
    objectID            ObjectID,
        measurementTime     MeasurementTimeOffset,
        timeConfidence      Common.TimeConfidence,
    pos                 PositionOffsetXYZ,
    posConfidence       Common.PositionConfidenceSet,
    speed               Common.Speed,
    speedConfidence     Common.SpeedConfidence,
    speedZ              Common.Speed OPTIONAL,
    speedConfidenceZ    Common.SpeedConfidence OPTIONAL,
    heading             Common.Heading,
    headingConf         Common.HeadingConfidence,
    accel4way           Common.AccelerationSet4Way OPTIONAL,
    accCfdX             ProbeVehicleData.AccelerationConfidence OPTIONAL,
    accCfdY             ProbeVehicleData.AccelerationConfidence OPTIONAL,
    accCfdZ             ProbeVehicleData.AccelerationConfidence OPTIONAL,
    accCfdYaw           ProbeVehicleData.YawRateConfidence OPTIONAL,
    ...
}

DetectedVehicleData::= SEQUENCE {
    lights                  Common.ExteriorLights              OPTIONAL,
    vehAttitude             Attitude                    OPTIONAL,
    vehAttitudeConfidence   AttitudeConfidence          OPTIONAL,
    vehAngVel               AngularVelocity             OPTIONAL,
    vehAngVelConfidence     AngularVelocityConfidence   OPTIONAL,
    size                    Common.VehicleSize                 OPTIONAL,
    height                  Common.VehicleHeight               OPTIONAL,
    vehicleSizeConfidence   VehicleSizeConfidence       OPTIONAL,
    vehicleClass            Common.BasicVehicleClass           OPTIONAL,
    classConf               ClassificationConfidence    OPTIONAL,
    ...
}

DetectedVRUData::= SEQUENCE {
    basicType       PersonalSafetyMessage.PersonalDeviceUserType  OPTIONAL,
    propulsion      PersonalSafetyMessage.PropelledInformation    OPTIONAL,
    attachment      PersonalSafetyMessage.Attachment              OPTIONAL,
    radius          PersonalSafetyMessage.AttachmentRadius        OPTIONAL
}

DetectedObstacleData::= SEQUENCE {
    obstSize            ObstacleSize,
    obstSizeConfidence  ObstacleSizeConfidence
}

ObjectType::= ENUMERATED{
    unknown (0),
    vehicle (1),
    vru     (2),
    animal  (3),
    ...
}

ObjectID ::= INTEGER (0..65535)

ClassificationConfidence ::= INTEGER (0..101)

MeasurementTimeOffset ::= INTEGER (-1500..1500) 
PositionOffsetXYZ ::= SEQUENCE {
    offsetX     ObjectDistance,
    offsetY     ObjectDistance,
    offsetZ     ObjectDistance OPTIONAL
}

ObjectDistance ::=INTEGER(-32767..32767) 
Attitude ::= SEQUENCE {
    pitch   PitchDetected,
    roll    RollDetected,
    yaw     YawDetected
}

PitchDetected ::= INTEGER (-7200..7200)
        
RollDetected ::= INTEGER (-14400..14400)
        
YawDetected ::= INTEGER (-14400..14400)
        
AttitudeConfidence ::= SEQUENCE {
    pitchConfidence     Common.HeadingConfidence,
    rollConfidence      Common.HeadingConfidence,
    yawConfidence       Common.HeadingConfidence
}

AngularVelocity ::= SEQUENCE {
    pitchRate   PitchRate,
    rollRate    RollRate
}

PitchRate ::= INTEGER (-32767..32767)
        
RollRate ::= INTEGER (-32767..32767)
        
AngularVelocityConfidence ::= SEQUENCE {
    pitchRateConfidence PitchRateConfidence OPTIONAL,
    rollRateConfidence RollRateConfidence OPTIONAL
}

PitchRateConfidence ::= ENUMERATED {
    unavailable (0),         degSec-100-00 (1),     degSec-010-00 (2),     degSec-005-00 (3),     degSec-001-00 (4),     degSec-000-10 (5),     degSec-000-05 (6),     degSec-000-01 (7)  }

RollRateConfidence ::= ENUMERATED {
    unavailable (0),         degSec-100-00 (1),     degSec-010-00 (2),     degSec-005-00 (3),     degSec-001-00 (4),     degSec-000-10 (5),     degSec-000-05 (6),     degSec-000-01 (7)  }

VehicleSizeConfidence ::= SEQUENCE {
    vehicleWidthConfidence      SizeValueConfidence,
    vehicleLengthConfidence     SizeValueConfidence,
    vehicleHeightConfidence     SizeValueConfidence OPTIONAL
}

ObstacleSize ::= SEQUENCE {
    width   SizeValue,
    length  SizeValue,
    height  SizeValue   OPTIONAL
}

ObstacleSizeConfidence ::= SEQUENCE {
    widthConfidence     SizeValueConfidence,
    lengthConfidence    SizeValueConfidence,
    heightConfidence    SizeValueConfidence     OPTIONAL
}

SizeValue ::= INTEGER (0..1023) 
SizeValueConfidence ::= ENUMERATED {
    unavailable (0),
    size-100-00 (1),        size-050-00 (2),        size-020-00 (3),        size-010-00 (4),        size-005-00 (5),        size-002-00 (6),        size-001-00 (7),        size-000-50 (8),        size-000-20 (9),        size-000-10 (10),       size-000-05 (11),       size-000-02 (12),       size-000-01 (13)    }

END
